# Santa pass 2026-10-14 (PT-50, prop-tools final per-item verification)

- Date: 2026-10-14 to 2026-10-15
- Tree: 86520a63 (branch prop-engine-audit-and-tools, clean working tree apart from the santa output files)
- Reviewer: one independent reviewer, phase 2
- Scope: every `| PT-` row of the PLAN.md work-package table with status done or implemented (308 rows), less PT-50; skipped by status: PT-13b, PT-35b, PT-64b, PT-72 (blocked), PT-35, PT-95 (todo), PT-30 (split; its done children PT-30a1, PT-30b, PT-30c, PT-30d are rows here), PT-30a2 (partial), PT-58f (absorbed into PT-62e), PT-70 (conditional).
- Method: each package's section in packages.md, HANDOFF.md or video-550k-packages.md was parsed for the source and test paths it names (braces and globs expanded, only existing files kept); test files tagged with the package ID were added; a package with neither takes its parent package's tests (for example PT-05d from PT-05), and PT-76, PT-79 and PT-99 take the tests tagged with their F items. All 746 resulting test files ran from the repo root with `bunx vitest run <files> --maxWorkers=3`, 40 files per command, one command at a time: 746 files pass, 0 fail, no test over 20 s. The fingerprint is sha256 over the sorted `shasum -a 256` lines of the package's source files plus its test files (sources fall back to the tagged tests' direct `~/` imports when the section names none); the full file lists are in `verify-hashes-2026-10-14.json`.
- Depth: the router ownership audit (below, PT-05 row), the round budget (PT-79) and UsdCents (PT-01) were traced by hand. For the other rows the evidence is the package's own tests passing and the tagged test titles; their code was not re-traced line by line, which is the limit of this pass. Rows whose tests come from a parent package are weaker evidence and say so.

## Summary

- 308 rows: 307 CONFIRMED by phase 2, 1 BLOCKED (PT-118, tracker only). Phase 3 (the deep trace, 2026-10-15) escalated 12 packages; 9 were fixed in their own commits and 3 recorded (see "Escalation outcomes" at the end).
- 64 rows rest on their parent package's tests (no test of their own was found by path or tag); they are confirmed only to the extent the parent's tests cover them.
- Test runs: 746 files, all pass, no single test over 20 s, no batch of 40 files over 15 s.
- Security checks this pass: every user-owned query in the propAccounts routers and `src/lib/prop-accounts/server` is scoped by the session userId; Zod `.output` on all 75 procedures and `.input` on all 60 that take input; no logging and no secret values in those files.
- Not done: any dev-server UI check, any live firm re-fetch. The line-by-line trace of the 205 money packages is done (phase 3) except PT-58a3, whose code was read against its spec only; many rows are partial traces that name the steps not read.

### Trace notes

- PT-01 (traced): `usdCents` (src/lib/prop-accounts/core/UsdCents.ts:90-97) rejects any amount that is not a whole safe integer; `usdCentsFromDollars` rejects non-finite input. Stored money is whole cents.
- PT-05 (traced): every user-owned query in the propAccounts routers and `PropAccountRepo`/`DpAdviceRepo`/`PropAccountQuotas` is scoped by the session userId (92 chains checked by a scan, the one bare outer select at PropAccountRepo.ts:367 reads a subquery filtered on userId at :363); repos are built only with `ctx.userId`; no `input.userId`; Zod `.output` on all 75 procedures, `.input` on all 60 with input; no logging or secrets. Ownership tests: userScoping.test.ts and per-router "write with id and user id" pins.
- PT-79 (traced): `assertRoundWithinBudget` (src/server/api/routers/propAccounts/round.ts:218-229) rejects `RoundBudgetExceeded` when `spentCents >= budgetCents` (bankroll/RoundBudget.ts:14, inclusive), unless `overrideRoundBudget`; a null budget never blocks; the message formats both figures from cents. It blocks the purchase after the budget is reached, not one that would cross it, which is what the title says. round.test.ts:127 and :162 assert the rejection.
- PT-58a1 (traced): `PAYOUT_TOLERANCE_CENTS = 100`, compared inclusively on the absolute difference (core/PayoutTolerance.ts); `firmReconciliation` (metrics/FirmReconciliation.ts) sums only Paid payouts paid on or before the statement date, per firm key, gross or net by the statement's basis, and compares each statement only with the previous one of the same basis. Fallback on a money path: on the net basis a payout with no net figure is counted at its gross (FirmReconciliation.ts:128-131). It is disclosed on the ledger page (GrossOnlyPayoutsNote: "the gross amount is counted as received"; the reconciliation cell adds "N paid without a net amount"), so not escalated. LOW: FirmReconciliation.test.ts:153 asserts only `grossOnlyCount`, not the summed cents under the substitution, so a change that dropped those payouts from the total would still pass that case.
- PT-92 (traced, fee-equivalent half): `feeEquivalentTradeRisk` = min(risk / evalDrawdown, 1) x retryFee in dollars, refusing a negative fee or risk and a non-positive drawdown, always tagged `NearFreshEvalApproximation` (economics/FeeEquivalentRisk.ts); riskDisplay.ts:37-70 carries that disclosure wherever the figure shows, including the labelled fallback from EV at stake (riskDisplay.test.ts:68, :93); FeeEquivalentRisk.test.ts:116-125 assert the full and half drawdown values. The instrument-mismatch half was checked by its tests only.
- PT-13 (traced, whole-contract placement): `wholeContractCount` floors (risk x 100 + the cent tolerance) / (one-contract risk x 100) (core/PositionSizing.ts:180-194), so float drift never drops a contract and a risk is never rounded up past the request; `wholeContractRisk` then places at least one contract (`Math.max(1, ...)`, decision T33), which can exceed a request below one contract; that case is refused for funded flat risk (U18, percentCushionSizing.test.ts:326) and on the lockout room (N-74). MES stays blocked (PT-13b).
- PT-12 (confirmed (traced)): One payout minimum: `minimumPayoutRequest` = the ladder minRequestAmount, else the plan minPayoutRequest (core/PayoutRequestPolicy.ts); `effectivePayoutRequest` = max(request, minimum) after a finite, above-$0 check; `FullRequestOnly` refuses a request below the minimum instead of raising it. Reconstruction defaults (cumulative payout, qualifying days, payouts taken, live start, peak order and the rest) each emit an `AssumptionKind`, and `ASSUMPTION_KIND_TEXT` is an exhaustive `Record` (advisor/Assumption.ts:116, 223-240), so every stand-in reaches the user as text. LOW: `optimize dp` prints the raised request (command.ts:228-235) without saying a below-minimum `--request-size` was raised to the plan minimum, unlike the retained cushion on the same line; the figure shown is the one used, so not escalated.
- PT-14 (confirmed (traced)): `simulateFromState` validates trials, eval days, attempts and the payout policy, then refuses an impossible start with the shared prefix (simulator/engine.ts:266-272); `FromStateSimOutputs` drops `expectedMonthlyNet`, `expectedMonthlyRealizedNet`, `costBreakdown` and the fresh estimates by type (simulator/types.ts:180-191), so a from-state run cannot report the fresh monthly net; it reports from-state cash with and without the credit over `fromStateWindowDays`. SimulateFromState.test.ts:224-330 refuse instant-funded eval starts, starts at the day cap, passed, busted or inactive starts and bad subscription days, and check the first day sees the seeded balance and threshold.
- PT-46 (confirmed (traced)): `evaluateDocumentedPayout` (advisor/PayoutReadiness.ts:174-184) is the engine tracker's own `evaluatePayout` under `FullRequestOnly` with the resolved retained cushion, so the documented payout uses every firm gate the engine uses and never offers a smaller request (FullRequestOnly refuses below the minimum, see PT-12); `isCycleEndingGate` is an exhaustive switch over `PayoutGate`. `payoutPath` defaults the request to `DEFAULT_PAYOUT_REQUEST_SIZE`, the documented rulebook request, not a stand-in for missing data. The live-trigger guard blocks a payout when payouts taken plus pending plus this one reach the per-account or firm-total trigger (`+ 1 >=`, :208-241).
- PT-47 (confirmed (traced)): The funded DP takes the retained cushion from `plan.resolveRetainedCushion` (FundedStateValue.ts:684), validates a request size as finite and > 0 (:687-694), carries the payout policy, lets a request size widen the post-payout baseline grid (`isBaselineAlwaysTheFloor` false once a size is set, :862-866) and seeds trackers through the shared `restoreFundedCycleTracker` (:1441). optimize dp hands it the effective request (PT-12 note). FundedStateValuePayoutPolicy.test.ts pins the unchanged defaults, the request on the baseline, the credit capped at the request and FullRequestOnly when the withdrawable cannot cover it (describes at :265-532).
- PT-19 (confirmed (traced)): The funded advice context passes `fundedConsistencyCeiling(account)` and its note (advisor/FundedSizingAdvisor.ts:806-812; CopyGroupSizing.ts:648-668), and `ruleContextAt` takes the tighter of the day ceiling and the consistency ceiling for funded (RuleContext.ts:188-193); the ceiling is the rule's `maxDayProfitBeforeViolation` of the cycle profit since the last payout (FundedConsistencyCeiling.ts), none when the cycle has no profit yet (disclosed in DifferenceReasons.ts:30-32). No advisor or advice-panel file mentions Kelly. Engine optima and placement trace to PT-13 and PT-47.
- PT-48 (escalate): MEDIUM. The documented policy the engine simulates does not apply the funded consistency ceiling that the advice card applies. `documentedDayRisk` builds its `RuleContextCaps` with only instrument, personal caps, personal DLL and placeable minimum (advisor/policy/DocumentedDayRisk.ts:104-116), so `ruleContextAt` resolves `consistencyCeiling ?? null` to no ceiling (RuleContext.ts:191), and the flat funded branch (`flatDayPolicy`, documentedPolicySimInputs.ts:79-84) has none either; the advisor passes the ceiling (FundedSizingAdvisor.ts:806-812). Failure scenario: on a funded plan with a funded consistency rule (Alpha Zero/Standard 40%, TopStep Consistency XFA, Tradeify Select Flex), a trader with $1,500 of cycle profit is told by the daily card to stop at the ceiling (about $1,000 more at 40%), while the projection, next-slot ranking and payout-size figures labelled "under the documented policy" keep trading past it, so more cycles violate consistency and payouts are timed and sized for a policy the user was told not to follow. The engine still enforces the rule at the payout, so the projection likely understates payout speed; PT-48 step 5 specified the flat funded policy this way, so the fix may be a disclosure rather than code. No test pins either behaviour for a consistency plan.
- PT-103 (confirmed (traced)): ReconstructionRoundTrip.test.ts:1122-1290 capture states from seeded engine funded, eval and live runs and assert the rebuilt state, cycle, cushion, daily loss limit and contract limit equal the engine values (`expectFundedRoundTrip`), including a late-lock plan whose payout floor depends on the peak order (from the floor at the last payout), every `PayoutFloorEffect` after a payout, funded resets, and live starts other than the builder start. TopStep live is left out of the live round trip because its reserve is hidden state; reconstruction discloses the default start with `LiveStartBalanceDefaulted` (exhaustive assumption text, see PT-12).
- PT-106 (confirmed (traced)): Breach half traced: `projectNextPayout` counts a trial as a payout-caused breach only when it busted on the day of its first payout (`stage === Busted && firstPayoutDay === daysElapsed`, advisor/NextPayoutProjection.ts:157-162), and both that rate and the loss before a first payout are shares of all trials with binomial standard errors (:168-198); the account value column and the from-state model label them that way ("The first payout caused a breach", accountValueColumns.ts:505-513; accountFromStateModel.ts:409-416), with the day figures marked as averaged over paying trials. The continuation-under-the-scored-policy half was checked by its tests only.
- PT-55 (confirmed (traced)): Bankroll timeline (portfolioTimeline/bankrollTimeline.ts): capacity is min(bankroll max concurrent accounts, `plan.maxFundedAccounts`) (:172-175); a purchase needs open slots, cash after reservations >= one attempt cost, and room in the monthly and round budgets, all inclusive (:398-411); path ruin is cash below one attempt cost with nothing open or pending (:238-245), reported as `pathRuin` = ruined trials / trials (:333) and printed by `prop bankroll project` (project.ts:174); payouts land after `payoutLagDays` (:385). LOW: `attemptCost` = initial eval fee + activation fee (:169-171), so on plans with an activation fee (Apex $90) the purchase gate and the ruin line require the activation up front although it is paid only after a pass; this overstates path ruin slightly and is conservative.

Stopped after PT-55 on context budget (phase 3 resume 2): traced so far PT-01, PT-05, PT-79, PT-58a1, PT-92, PT-13, PT-12, PT-14, PT-46, PT-47, PT-19, PT-48, PT-103, PT-106, PT-55. Next in the queue: PT-80, then the rest of the selection by money impact. The other selected rows keep their phase 2 verdict (tests pass, not traced).

## Second review (main session, 2026-10-15)

- PT-48 escalation: confirmed. `documentedDayRisk` builds its caps without `consistencyCeiling` and ignores the `fundedCycle` argument the simulator passes (`simulator/day.ts:114`), and the flat funded branch has no ceiling. Not fixed tonight: most firms carry a funded consistency rule, so the fix changes every documented-policy figure on those plans and reverses PT-48 step 5. ## Deep trace escalations

- PT-12l, src/lib/prop-calculator/simulator/engine.ts:1124-1144 (and :498-510): when copied MFF Pro accounts pass the $100,000 per-user lifetime cap together, payouts are cut to cap / copies but every copy keeps its `horizonCredit`, which `closeoutCredit` only zeroes once that single copy reaches the cap itself (core/FundedPayoutCycle.ts:266-268). Scenario: MFF Pro with `copyAccounts` 3 over a long funded horizon, a trial whose copies each earn $40,000 ($120,000 combined) and are still alive at the horizon: payouts are cut to $33,333 each, yet each copy adds a full request credit (for example $2,000 x 3) to `expectedMonthlyNet`, which the payout sweep, next slot and strategy lab rank on; nothing more can be withdrawn under the cap. Fix: zero (or clamp to the remaining pool room) the credit of a trial whose pooled cap binds. Severity LOW (one plan, copies above 1, only trials that reach the cap; the credit-free column is correct).
- PT-56, src/lib/prop-accounts/metrics/RealizedAttemptEconomics.ts:166-177: `payoutsPerPaidFundedOf` takes `Math.min(count, PAYOUT_COUNT_CAP)` (10, the histogram tail bucket) per account before averaging, so the realized "payouts per paid funded account" on the attempt economics card is capped, and `FunnelDiagnostic` (FunnelDiagnostic.ts:171, fed by overviewModel.ts:4707-4738) compares it with the uncapped engine mean `payoutCountSum / reachedFundedCount` (simulator/engine.ts:883-884). Scenario: two paid funded accounts with 20 and 14 Paid payouts inside the horizon (frequent-payout plans such as daily or weekly payout rules) show 10 payouts per paid funded instead of 17, and the diagnostic reports a large negative dollar gap at the payouts-per-paid stage that does not exist, ranking it as the stage costing the most. Fix: average the raw counts; keep the cap only for the distribution buckets. Severity MEDIUM (wrong displayed figure and a wrong stage ranking for active traders; EV per attempt itself uses net cash and is unaffected).
- PT-65a, src/lib/prop-calculator/advisor/value/TradeValueSwing.ts:95-105 (with ValueChain.ts:231-247 and simulator/trial.ts:330-372): when the proposed eval trade would pass on a win, `afterWin` is the value of a fresh funded account started through a funded-start trial with `evalCost: 0`, so the activation fee the trader pays on passing is never charged, while `now` (an eval-state run) charges `activationFee` when its trials pass (trial.ts:297-300). Scenario: an eval account $300 from target with a plan that bills an activation fee on pass (for example $149): for a $150 risk at 1:2 the card shows dEV(win) about $149 too high, and the risk-candidate table gives every candidate large enough to pass on this win a bonus of p x $149 over smaller ones. No caller nets it (only the bust branch is netted, `netOfReplacementFee`). Fix: subtract `activationFee(plan.fees, discounts)` from the after-win value when the win passes, beside the replacement-fee netting. Severity LOW (one trade per eval, bounded by the activation fee; zero on plans without one).
- PT-104 (step 8), src/lib/prop-calculator/advisor/LiveSizingAdvisor.ts:241 and :257: the live rule context keeps `placeableMinimum: ONE_CENT` even with an entered instrument and stop. `nextTrade` only floors a cap-reduced rung to whole contracts when the placeable minimum is above one cent (DocumentedRule.ts:314-319) and only stops for the day below that minimum (:323-328), so on a live account a rung capped by the cushion, the daily loss room or the ceiling is shown at its exact dollar room, not in whole contracts, and a percent rung below one contract is still offered (only the BelowOneContract flag marks it). Scenario: live MNQ at a 20-point stop ($40 a contract) with $100 of daily loss room left: the card shows a $100 rung; a trader who places 3 contracts risks $120 and can breach the live limit, where the funded card would show $80 (2 contracts). Fix: build the live context with `advisorPlaceableMinimum(this.input.positionSizing)` as the spec step says. Severity MEDIUM (live money; only with an entered stop and a binding cap).
- PT-68b, src/app/(app)/prop-calculator/accounts/_components/advice/personalRuleOptions.ts:179-182 (and :165-168, :256-260) with src/lib/prop-accounts/core/JsonbReaders.ts:38-40: stored personal rules that fail `personalRulesSchema` are read as null, so the advice, the account-from-state values, the readiness board and the overview requests are computed with no personal max trades, daily profit cap, personal DLL, payout override or retained cushion. Scenario: an account whose stored `personal_rules` jsonb no longer parses (a later schema tightening, a bad manual edit) with a $300 personal DLL gets advice sized to the documented firm DLL. The detail page shows a read-only notice that the personal rules are not valid (AccountDetailView.tsx:373, :404-411, PlanKey.ts:137-139) and the overview counts the account in its data-quality line, but the advice panel and its figures never say they ignore the personal limits. Severity LOW (partly disclosed on the page; the safe choice is to refuse personal-limit-dependent advice for that account or name the dropped limits in the panel).
- PT-59b, src/lib/prop-accounts/conduct/TiltVarianceSplit.ts:85 (summed in overviewModel.ts readyTiltVarianceCard): a violation whose `costCents` is null (the field is optional, propAccounts.ts:944, :958) is skipped, so its month shows a violation cost and a "net without violations" as if it cost $0, with no count of unpriced violations beside the row. Scenario: three oversized trades in March recorded without a cost show $0 violation cost and net without violations equal to net cash, reading as no tilt cost. Severity LOW (the figure errs toward understating the tilt cost; the fix is a per-row count of unpriced violations, or n/a).
- PT-58a3 (found while tracing PT-22d), src/lib/prop-accounts/alerts/BankrollLossRiskAboveThresholdRule.ts:21-26 with bankroll/RealizedLossRisk.ts:94-106: when the realized mean net per attempt is at or below zero, `realizedLossRisk` returns `reason: NoPositiveEdge` with a null batch loss probability and the rule returns no alert, and no other alert rule covers a non-positive realized edge (alerts/AlertEvaluator.ts:40-75). Scenario: 20 decided attempts averaging -$120 net with a 25% loss-risk threshold set gives no alert, while +$5 average with a 40% batch loss probability does alert; the trader is warned in the milder case and silent in the worst one. Severity MEDIUM (the bankroll page shows the no-positive-edge state, but the alert channel the threshold was set for stays quiet; the fix is to alert, with the no-positive-edge wording, once the sample is adequate).
- PT-12k, src/lib/prop-accounts/advice/SnapshotAdapter.ts:283-288: `elapsedDaysSinceAttemptStart` is calendar days since purchase plus one, but it becomes `state.elapsedDays`, which the engine treats as trading sessions (simulator/engine.ts:1202-1209 subtracts it from the session day cap; core/EvalStartState.ts refuses a start once it reaches the cap, 'every traded day is also an elapsed session'; core/FeeSchedule.ts:167-173 bills it at 21 sessions a month; EvalSizingAdvisor passes it as `subscriptionElapsedDays`). Scenario: an Apex 50K eval (30 calendar days of access, modeled as round(30 x 5 / 7) = 21 sessions, firms/apex/ApexTraderFunding.ts:76-79) bought Thursday 2026-10-01 with a snapshot on 2026-10-14 has 10 sessions behind it and about 11 left; the engine sets elapsed 14 and simulates 7 remaining sessions, so the from-state pass odds, eval values and the from-state ladder search run under a third less time (time pressure favours larger rungs); from 2026-10-21 (elapsed 21, about 15 sessions in, 6 left) every from-state eval run is refused as 'no session under the eval-day cap'. Non-capped evals lose about 2 sessions of horizon per week the same way. Severity HIGH (eval advice and values on every modeled eval account, worst on time-limited plans). A weekday counter already exists (`weekdaysInRange`, core/lib/isoDate.ts:123-139).
- PT-62 (step 4, PT-62c), src/app/(app)/prop-calculator/accounts/rounds/roundsModel.ts:419-430 with :390-401 and portfolioTimeline/bankrollTimeline.ts:219-226: `nextRoundBankrollPolicy` sets `reinvestFraction: 0`, and the timeline adds only `reinvestFraction x payout` to cash (the rest goes to `withdrawn`), so in both options the timeline cash only ever falls by fees. `medianMonthlyNetOf` is (P50 cash at the end minus P50 cash at the start) / days x 21, so the 'Median monthly net (P50)' on the Next round card (RoundsView.tsx:554-557) is minus the fee spend rate and never counts a payout, and the path ruin there is 'the start budget ran out' because payouts never replenish it. Scenario: a closed round of 3 x $150 evals that earned $4,000 in payouts with the scale gate Ready: option A ($450) and option B ($4,450, capped by the available bankroll) both show a negative median monthly net, B's more negative because it buys more cards, so the card always recommends repeating (B is never recommended unless its spend is lower), whatever the plan's edge. The tests feed synthetic rising cash (roundsModel.test.ts:780-854) that the real request never produces. Fix: measure the monthly net from payouts minus spend (`payoutP50 - cumulativeSpendP50`, or cash plus withdrawn) or run the options with reinvestFraction 1. Severity HIGH (the repeat-or-scale figure and recommendation for every closed round are wrong in sign and never favour scaling).
- PT-65 (step 6), src/lib/prop-calculator/advisor/value/FundedValueEstimate.ts:90-98 with :120-131: the 'what your own n accounts could show by chance' payouts-per-account range uses `standardDeviationOfDistribution(out.fundedPayoutCountDistribution, mean)`, but that distribution folds every count of 10 or more into bucket 10 (simulator/engine.ts:72, :776-778) while `mean` is the uncapped `payoutsPerFundedAccount`. Scenario: a funded plan that pays every week or two over the 252-day default horizon with a true mean of 14 payouts and most mass at 12 to 16: the histogram puts that mass at 10, so the SD term sees (10 - 14)^2 for it and the range around 14 is several payouts too wide (the true spread is far smaller), so a trader whose own accounts average 9 payouts is told that is within chance when the model is off. Fix: take the SD from the uncapped moments (`payoutsPerFundedAccount` SE x sqrt(funded trials), the way the dollar range does at :76-81). Severity MEDIUM (wrong displayed range on the funded value card for frequent-payout plans; the same capped-histogram family as PT-56).
- PT-80 (step 5), src/lib/prop-accounts/bankroll/RealizedLossRisk.ts:181-201 with :70-86: `pooledAttemptPaysRate` counts a funded attempt as a success only when `paidCountWithinHorizon(entry, funded.on, horizonDays) > 0`, and `horizonDays` is `toFirstPayoutDays`, the n-weighted MEAN measured days to first payout. Every ended account is in the cohort regardless of age, so an ended account whose first payout came after the mean lag counts as 'no payout', while `perAttemptNetCents` for the same account counts all its payouts in the net. Scenario: four ended funded accounts, one attempt each, first paid payouts on days 10, 20, 30 and 60 after funding; the mean lag is 30, so the attempt-pays rate is 3/4 = 0.75 instead of 4/4 = 1.0, and the bankroll card's and CLI's realized P(no payout in N attempts) = (1 - 0.75)^N is 0.25^N instead of 0. With a right-skewed lag distribution the understatement of the pays rate is systematic (every payer slower than the mean is lost). Direction is conservative (overstates no-payout risk), but it is a wrong figure the user sizes budgets on. Severity MEDIUM.

### Escalation outcomes (main session, 2026-10-15)
